Result: 发现四个主要结果：1）模型间差异主要由方向性反应偏差驱动（占81-90%变异），而非目标特质（仅9-16%，人类为9-16%）；2）偏差随模型能力提升而降低但未被消除；3）测量工具的表面可靠性几乎完全由反应正交性预测；4）模型呈现的心理档案随测量项目变化，可通过项目选择人为制造特定画像。

Conclusion: LLM的表面心理档案是测量工具产生的伪影，而非模型本身的稳定属性。借用的人类心理测量工具缺乏对LLM的内在有效性。研究呼吁开发以反应正交性为中心的专用LLM评估方法，而非直接套用人类心理测量工具。

Abstract: Psychological instruments designed for humans are increasingly used to assign large language models (LLMs) stable psychological profiles that affect their usability, safety assessment, and use as proxies for human participants in research. Using a formal psychometric framework, we show that these profiles are largely a measurement artifact. Administering a battery of personality and risk-preference instruments spanning self-reports and behavioral tasks to 56 instruction-tuned LLMs alongside large human reference samples, we report four findings. First, differences between models are driven not by the traits an instrument targets but by a directional response bias, a tendency to respond toward one end of the scale, or one labeled option, regardless of item content; a variance decomposition attributes 81-90% of between-model variation to this bias, against 9-16% in humans. Second, the bias declines with model capability but is not eliminated by it. Third, because bias rather than trait drives responding, an instrument's apparent reliability is almost entirely predicted by its response orthogonality, a term we coin for the proportion of items for which trait and bias point in opposite directions. Fourth, the profile a model appears to have shifts with the items used and can be manufactured through item selection. These results demonstrate that the apparent psychological profiles of LLMs are artifacts of the instrument used to measure them, not properties of the models themselves. As instruments borrowed from human psychology are rarely fully orthogonal and may inherently lack validity for LLMs, we call for dedicated assessments centered on response orthogonality.

</details>


### [180] [Beyond Accuracy: Measuring Logical Compliance of Predictive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20208)
*Guillaume Olivier Delplanque,Pierre Genevès,Nabil Layaïda,Zephirin Faure*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了规则违反分数(RVS)这一新评估指标，用于量化预测模型对逻辑规则的遵守程度，可作为预测准确性的补充指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习评估指标（如排名质量、预测误差、分类准确率）仅衡量预测与真实值的匹配程度，无法评估模型输出是否遵守预定义的逻辑或领域约束。在医疗、金融和自主系统等高风险应用中，逻辑一致性与预测准确性同样重要，但缺乏标准指标来衡量这一维度。

Method: 引入规则违反分数(RVS)，这是一种补充评估指标，独立于预测准确性来量化预测模型对给定逻辑规则集的遵守程度。RVS区分处理硬规则（严格约束）和软规则（统计规律），可在任何数据集和任何在关系词汇表上表达的预测模型上评估，并能使用自动生成的SQL查询来计算Horn规则。除评估模型外，RVS还可评估训练数据集的逻辑一致性并帮助识别定义不当的规则。

Result: 在涵盖知识图谱链接预测和关系回归的三个基准上评估了RVS，包括基于规则、基于嵌入和神经符号预测模型。结果显示，两个达到相当预测准确性的模型可能表现出显著不同的逻辑合规水平，揭示了标准指标未能捕捉的模型行为差异。

Conclusion: RVS作为预测性能指标的有效补充，能够揭示传统评估方法无法检测的模型行为差异，为高风险应用中的模型评估提供了逻辑一致性维度的新视角。

Abstract: Machine learning models are predominantly evaluated through predictive performance metrics such as ranking quality, prediction error, or classification accuracy. While these metrics effectively quantify how closely predictions match the ground truth, they do not assess whether model outputs respect predefined logical or domain-specific constraints. In high-stakes applications, including healthcare, finance, and autonomous systems, logical consistency can be as critical as predictive accuracy, yet no standard metric captures this dimension. We introduce the Rule Violation Score (RVS), a complementary evaluation metric that quantifies the extent to which a predictive model respects a given set of logical rules, independently of predictive accuracy. RVS treats hard rules (strict constraints) and soft rules (statistical regularities) differently, can be evaluated on any dataset and on any predictive model expressed over a relational vocabulary, and can be computed using SQL queries that are automatically generated for Horn rules. Beyond evaluating models, RVS can also evaluate the logical consistency of training datasets and help identify poorly defined rules. We evaluate RVS on three benchmarks covering knowledge graph link prediction and relational regression, including rule-based, embedding-based, and neuro-symbolic predictive models. Our results demonstrate that two models achieving comparable predictive accuracy can exhibit substantially different levels of logical compliance, revealing differences in model behavior that standard metrics fail to capture.

</details>


### [181] [Augmenting Game AI with Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20210)
*Alessandro Sestini,Joakim Bergdahl,Amir Baghi,Jean-Philippe Barrette-LaPierre,Florian Fuchs,Linus Gisslén*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于游戏AI的强化学习训练框架，旨在解决当前研究的局限性，推动机器学习在电子游戏行业的广泛应用，以创造更具真实感和沉浸感的游戏角色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统手工编码的游戏AI难以捕捉复杂的行为，导致角色缺乏真实感，容易打破玩家的沉浸体验。随着机器学习模型的引入，有望通过从游戏交互或玩家数据中学习来开发真正类人的角色行为，但当前研究存在局限性，阻碍了其在不同游戏类型中的广泛部署。

Method: 提出一个针对游戏AI和游戏开发需求定制的强化学习模型训练框架。该框架考虑了游戏AI的实际应用场景，展示了采用强化学习增强游戏AI的游戏案例，并描述了在现代游戏中部署面向玩家的机器学习智能体的实践方法。

Result: 成功构建了适用于游戏AI的强化学习训练框架，并通过实际游戏案例验证了其可行性，识别出了该领域的关键瓶颈和难题，这些难题为加速机器学习在游戏AI中的应用提供了有前景的研究方向。

Conclusion: 强化学习在游戏AI领域具有巨大潜力，通过建立合适的训练框架和解决关键技术瓶颈，可以加速机器学习在电子游戏行业的采用，从而创造出更具沉浸感和真实感的游戏角色，提升玩家的整体游戏体验。

Abstract: Immersion in video games depends not only on graphics, audio, and game mechanics, but also on the quality of in-game characters. Producing believable characters, or game AI, remains a significant challenge as behavioral complexity is hard to capture with hand-coded systems. Game AI is a source of immersion and engagement; however, the limitations stemming from the challenges of creating game AI often lead to frustration and the breaking of the illusion of realism within the game. The introduction of machine learning models opens the door to creating more believable, authentic, and relatable characters in games. The promise is that they either learn from interacting with the game, or from player data, to develop true human-like behavior. In this paper, we envision more applications of reinforcement learning for game AI in the future. For this to materialize, current research limitations are prohibitive to broad deployment across game genres. Therefore, we propose a framework for training reinforcement learning models with a set of requirements in mind that are suited towards game AI and game development. We present examples of games with reinforcement learning-augmented game AI and describe the practicalities of deploying player-facing machine learning agents in modern games. Furthermore, we identify bottlenecks and hard problems in these areas, which we believe offer promising research directions to accelerate the adoption of machine learning in game AI for the video game industry.

</details>


### [182] [QMFOL: Benchmarking Large Language Model Reasoning via Quantifiable Monadic First-Order Logic Test Case Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20227)
*Xinyi Zheng,Ling Shi,Tianlong Yu,Yongxin Zhao,Lorenz Goette,Kailong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了QMFOL框架，用于自动生成复杂度可控的一元一阶逻辑推理任务，并构建了包含2880个实例的QMFOLBench基准测试，揭示了模型性能随逻辑复杂度增加而下降、对标签类型和语义变化敏感等规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型推理能力评估基准存在两大局限：缺乏对逻辑复杂度的细粒度控制能力，以及难以在语义多样性与逻辑一致性之间取得平衡。随着模型推理能力（尤其是高风险决策中的演绎推理）的不断提升，亟需能够与时俱进且更精确的评估基准。

Method: 提出QMFOL自动化框架，首先使用合取和析取模式构建形式化逻辑结构，实现对推理深度、宽度、标签类型和干扰项的精确控制；随后通过大语言模型将逻辑结构翻译为自然语言，并利用外部证明器进行往返验证以确保逻辑一致性。基于该框架构建了QMFOLBench基准，包含2880个实例，涵盖960种配置。

Result: 对6个大型推理模型和2个大语言模型的评估显示：（1）随着逻辑复杂度提升，模型性能下降且计算开销增加；（2）模型在True标签任务上的表现优于False和Unknown标签任务；（3）模型对语义变化表现出敏感性。

Conclusion: QMFOL提供了一个可扩展且可靠的方法来构建复杂度可控的演绎推理基准，能够更精确地评估现代语言模型的推理能力，为未来推理模型的评估和发展提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have made significant progress in reasoning, particularly in deductive reasoning, which is crucial for high-stakes decision-making. As models improve, evaluation benchmarks should evolve to keep pace. However, existing benchmarks lack fine-grained control over logical complexity and struggle to balance semantic diversity with logical consistency.
  To address these issues, we propose QMFOL, an automated framework for generating monadic first-order logic reasoning tasks with quantifiable and controllable complexity. It constructs formal logical structures using conjunction and disjunction patterns, enabling precise control over reasoning depth, width, label types, and distractors. These structures are then translated into natural language via LLMs, with logical consistency ensured through round-trip verification using an external prover. Based on our framework, we build QMFOLBench, a benchmark comprising 2880 instances with 960 configurations across diverse logical and semantic dimensions. Evaluations on six large reasoning models (LRMs) and two LLMs show that performance degrades and computational overhead increases with rising logical complexity. Models perform better on True-labeled tasks than on False or Unknown ones, and exhibit sensitivity to semantic variation. Overall, QMFOL offers a scalable and reliable approach for constructing deductive reasoning benchmarks with controllable complexity, enabling more precise evaluation of reasoning capabilities in modern language models.

</details>


### [183] [Thermodynamic Measure of Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20231)
*Ishanu Chattopadhyay*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于热力学的普适智能度量方法：智能被定义为对“罕见但有效未来”的合法放大。核心发现是递归自模拟结构是实现高热力学智能的必要且近充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨智能是否可以被度量，以及如何在一个统一的框架下量化从被动物质、反馈控制器、大语言模型到人类等不同系统的智能水平。

Method: 1. 将智能定义为系统提高某些未来状态概率的能力——这些状态在被动动力学下罕见但仍符合领域约束（罕见但有效未来）；2. 引入递归自模拟概念，即系统在建模世界时必须将自身纳入模型，从而递归地模拟包含自身行动的未来轨迹；3. 建立热力学度量指标"罕见有效提升"（rare-valid lift）；4. 证明必要性定理与条件近充分性定理。

Result: 证明了两个核心定理：（1）必要性——如果系统要实现高罕见有效提升，内部模拟必须高保真地识别罕见有效未来；（2）条件近充分性——当罕见有效保真度高且模拟包含有效策略时，可实现的提升接近致动极限最优值。这表明递归自模拟是实现高热力学智能的核心机制。

Conclusion: 递归自模拟不仅是智能的一个合理特征，在给定假设下更是实现高热力学智能的必要且近充分条件。该框架使智能可以在普适尺度上进行度量，为比较从物理系统、人工智能模型到生物智能体的智能水平提供了统一的理论基础。

Abstract: Can intelligence be measured? We propose that intelligence can be defined as the lawful amplification of rare but valid futures: a system increases the probability of outcomes that would be unlikely under passive dynamics but remain admissible under the constraints of the domain. We start with the premise that an intelligent system must model the world and its own place within it. Because the system is part of the world it models, this leads naturally to recursive self-simulation: the system represents futures in which its own actions are part of the trajectory. Our central results give a necessity statement and a conditional near-sufficiency statement connecting this architecture to a precise thermodynamic measure of lawful amplification of rare-valid futures: high rare-valid lift is impossible unless the internal simulation identifies rare-valid futures with high fidelity; conversely, when rare-valid fidelity is high and the simulation contains an effective policy, the achievable lift approaches the actuation-limited optimum. Thus recursive self-simulation is not merely a plausible feature of intelligence but, under the stated assumptions, is necessary and nearly sufficient for high thermodynamic intelligence. The resulting framework makes intelligence measurable on a universal scale, from passive matter and feedback controllers, large language models, and humans as text generators to Maxwell-demon-like information engines.

</details>


### [184] [Navigating Unreliable Parametric and Contextual Knowledge: Explicit Knowledge Conflict Resolution for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20245)
*Huang Peng,Jiuyang Tang,Weixin Zeng,Hao Xu,Xiang Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MACR框架，通过多智能体推理机制主动解决大语言模型中内部参数知识与外部信息之间的知识冲突，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理LLM内部知识与外部信息冲突时，通常假设某一来源可靠并直接优先采用，忽视了双方都可能出错的情况，缺乏主动解决不一致性的机制。

Method: 提出MACR框架：1) 使用改进的语义熵量化模型对答案的置信度，据此选择外化内部知识或检索外部知识生成基础上下文；2) 构建归纳式多智能体推理框架，包含三个专门智能体分别负责规则归纳、冲突分析和一致性解决。

Result: MACR在多个基准测试上显著优于最先进的基线方法，同时能够提供可解释的显式冲突解决方案。

Conclusion: MACR突破了传统二元选择范式，通过显式的冲突解决机制和多智能体协作推理，有效解决了LLM知识冲突问题，提升了模型性能和可解释性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved strong performance across a wide range of language-based tasks by leveraging both extensive parametric knowledge and in-context learning ability, enabling them to incorporate external information provided in the input prompt. However, the integration of external knowledge can introduce conflicts, not only between the model's internal parametric knowledge and the external information, but also among multiple pieces of external contexts. Existing approaches typically assume that either the model or the provided context is reliable, overlooking the possibility that both sources may contain errors, and avoid conflicts by privileging one source over the other, rather than actively resolving inconsistencies. To address these limitations, we propose a novel framework MACR for LLM knowledge conflict resolution that moves beyond the conventional binary choice paradigm and incorporates an explicit conflict-resolution mechanism based on a multi-agent reasoning approach. Specifically, we first propose an adaptive knowledge assessment and retrieval approach that employs a modified semantic entropy measure to quantify an LLM's confidence in its answer to a given query. Based on this confidence estimation, MACR either externalizes the model's internal knowledge as textual representations or retrieves relevant external knowledge when internal knowledge is insufficient, generating basic contexts for subsequent reasoning. Then we introduce an inductive multi-agent reasoning framework with three specialized agents that, respectively, induce explicit rules, analyze potential conflicts, and resolve inconsistencies across all available contexts. Empirical results demonstrate that MACR significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across benchmarks, while also providing interpretable resolutions of explicit conflicts.

</details>


### [185] [Confidence-Aware Automated Assessment of Student-Drawn Scientific Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20264)
*Luyang Fang,Yingchuan Zhang,Jongchan Park,Zhaoji Wang,Ping Ma,Xiaoming Zhai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于视觉模型的中学生科学绘图自动评分方法，通过置信度感知框架实现了高置信度样本的自动评分与不确定样本的人工审核的有效平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学生绘制的科学绘图被广泛用于评估其概念理解，但评分过程依赖专家人工判断复杂的视觉表征，使得大规模评估在课堂环境中成本高昂且难以持续。

Method: 采用参数高效适应的视觉Transformer（ViT）模型，并提出置信度感知评分框架，通过测试时预测分布推导响应级别的置信度，实现选择性自动化评分。

Result: 在六项NGSS对齐的中学评估项目上的实验表明，该方法提高了评分可靠性，并在自动化覆盖率与评分风险之间实现了实用的权衡。

Conclusion: 置信度感知方法在教育评估中具有重要价值，能够支持可信的自动评分系统，通过智能分配人工审核资源提高评估效率。

Abstract: Student-generated drawings are widely used in science education to assess learners' conceptual understanding in modeling-based tasks aligned with the Next Generation Science Standards (NGSS). However, scoring such drawings requires expert human judgment to interpret complex visual representations, making large-scale assessment costly to implement and sustain in classroom settings. In this work, we study automated scoring of student-generated scientific drawings using a vision-based model. We evaluate a Vision Transformer (ViT) with parameter-efficient adaptation and propose a confidence-aware scoring framework that derives response-level confidence from test-time predictive distributions. This confidence signal enables selective automation by scoring high-confidence responses automatically while deferring uncertain cases for human review. Experiments on six NGSS-aligned middle school assessment items show that the proposed approach improves scoring reliability while supporting a practical trade-off between automated coverage and scoring risk, highlighting the value of confidence-aware methods for trustworthy educational assessment.

</details>


### [186] [Lagrange: An Open-Vocabulary, Energy-Based Sparse Framework for Generalized End-to-End Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20274)
*Shihao Ji,HongXi Li,Zihui Song,Mingyu Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Lagrange框架，通过Masked Latent Fields实现开放词汇、计算稀疏的自动驾驶，平衡了表征效率与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶范式在表征效率和泛化能力之间存在两难：稠密模型（如占用网络）计算开销大且缺乏高层语义推理；稀疏查询规划器依赖闭集定义，难以处理分布外事件；视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的离散token生成与车辆连续高频控制需求冲突。

Method: 利用视觉-语言模型（VLM）将类别无关的目标提议编码为连续语义视觉令牌；引入意图驱动的掩码交叉注意力模块时序过滤无关实体；将注意力令牌解码为空间坐标上的隐式连续能量场；通过在此能量场上进行拉格朗日作用量最小化决策，严格遵循车辆运动学约束并执行避撞。

Result: 在标准数据集（nuScenes）和长尾数据集（CODA）上的广泛离线评估表明，该方法在开放世界环境下具备鲁棒性、可解释性和运动学可行性。

Conclusion: Lagrange为开放世界自动驾驶提供了一个兼具鲁棒性、可解释性和运动学可行性的有前景框架，有效解决了现有方法在泛化与效率之间的权衡问题。

Abstract: Scaling end-to-end autonomous driving to complex, open-world environments requires perceptual models that generalize to anomalous scenarios and planners that produce kinematically valid trajectories. Existing paradigms face a distinct dichotomy between representational efficiency and generalization capacity. Dense models (e.g., occupancy networks), while geometrically robust, incur critical computational bottlenecks and struggle with high-level semantic reasoning. Conversely, sparse, query-based planners are efficient but reliant on closed-set definitions, rendering them vulnerable to out-of-distribution (OOD) events. Although recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models offer open-vocabulary reasoning, their autoregressive, discrete token generation fundamentally conflicts with the continuous, high-frequency control requirements of vehicle dynamics. To address this, we propose Lagrange, an open-vocabulary, computationally sparse driving framework based on Masked Latent Fields (MLF). Rather than relying on dense volumetric reconstructions or closed-set query mechanisms, Lagrange exploits Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to encode class-agnostic object proposals into continuous semantic visual tokens. We introduce an intent-driven masked cross-attention module that temporally filters irrelevant entities, decoding the attended tokens into an implicit continuous energy field defined over spatial coordinates. By framing decision-making as a Lagrangian action minimization problem spanning this energy field, we enforce strict compliance with vehicle kinematics while executing collision avoidance. Extensive offline evaluations on both standard (nuScenes) and long-tail (CODA) benchmarks demonstrate that Lagrange establishes a promising framework for robust, interpretable, and kinematically feasible open-world autonomy.

</details>


### [187] [Leveraging systems' non-linearity to tackle the scarcity of data in the design of Intelligent Fault Diagnosis Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20323)
*Giancarlo Santamato,Andrea Mattia Garavagno,Massimiliano Solazzi,Antonio Frisoli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度迁移学习和周期性多激励水平过程的智能故障诊断方法，有效解决了强数据稀缺条件下的振动故障诊断问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度迁移学习在构建智能故障诊断系统时仍然严重依赖大量标注数据，而在处理机械或结构故障时获取如此大量的数据具有挑战性，特别是在数据极度稀缺的情况下。

Method: 提出了一种周期性多激励水平过程，利用真实世界系统的内在非线性特性生成图像，通过预训练的卷积神经网络(CNN)进行故障诊断；同时提出了新的数据可视化方法和数据增强技术来解决IFDS设计过程中典型数据不足的问题。

Result: 在铁路受电弓结构上的实验验证为所提出方法的有效性提供了有力支持。

Conclusion: 该方法能够在强数据稀缺条件下有效设计基于振动的智能故障诊断系统，为解决实际工程中的数据稀缺问题提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Deep Transfer Learning (DTL) allows for the efficient building of Intelligent Fault Diagnosis Systems (IFDS). On the other hand, DTL methods still heavily rely on large amounts of labelled data. Obtaining such an amount of data can be challenging when dealing with machines or structures faults. This document proposes a novel approach to the design of vibration-based IFDS using DTL in condition of strong data scarcity. A periodic multi-excitation level procedure leveraging intrinsic non-linearities of real-world systems is used to produce images that can be conveniently analysed by pre-trained Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) to diagnose faults. A new data visualization method and its augmentation technique are proposed in this paper to tackle the typical lack of data encountered during the design of IFDS. Experimental validation on a railway pantograph structure provides effective support for the proposed method.

</details>


### [188] [SoftSkill: Behavioral Compression for Contextual Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20333)
*Xijia Tao,Yihua Teng,Xinyu Fu,Ziru Liu,Kecheng Chen,Yuzhi Zhao,Suiyun Zhang,Rui Liu,Lingpeng Kong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SoftSkill方法，将自然语言技能文件转换为紧凑的连续上下文对象，在冻结模型的基础上通过训练软delta来优化，在多个基准测试上显著优于传统提示方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Agent技能通常以自然语言Markdown文件形式部署，虽然具有可读性和可移植性，但在推理时需要冻结的语言模型将长文本转换为行为，效率低下。本文探讨是否可以将自然语言技能初始化为紧凑的连续上下文对象，在保持基础模型冻结的情况下通过可训练的软delta进行优化。

Method: 提出SoftSkill方法：一种冻结主干的方法，通过下一token预测来训练软技能，在推理时将其部署为潜在行为先验。该方法使用长度为32的SoftSkill前缀作为虚拟token，替代传统的大量Markdown技能token。

Result: 在Qwen3.5-4B模型上，长度为32的SoftSkill前缀相比无技能提示在SearchQA上提升8.3分，LiveMath上提升42.1分，DocVQA上提升1.3分。相比SkillOpt方法，在SearchQA上提升5.2分，LiveMath上提升12.5分，同时用少量虚拟token替代了数百到数千个Markdown技能token。在Agent执行场景中，稀疏轨迹模仿提供了有用信号，但尚未能稳健地压缩长视野程序行为。

Conclusion: 某些任务技能不应被视为在推理时重新解释的附加Markdown，而应被视为对冻结模型如何进入任务的紧凑潜在控制。这为技能表示和部署提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Agent skills are commonly deployed as natural-language Markdown files that encode answer policies, evidence-use habits, and task procedures. These files are readable and portable, but they are consumed indirectly: for each task instance, a frozen language model must translate a long textual artifact into generation-time behavior. This paper asks whether a natural-language skill can instead initialize a compact continuous context object, refined by a trainable soft delta while the base model remains frozen. We propose SoftSkill, a frozen-backbone method that tunes such soft skills with next-token prediction and deploys them as latent behavioral priors at inference time. In our main single-round setting, a length-32 SoftSkill prefix on Qwen3.5-4B improves over no-skill prompting by 8.3 points on SearchQA, 42.1 points on LiveMath, and 1.3 points on DocVQA. Relative to SkillOpt, SoftSkill improves accuracy by 5.2 points on SearchQA and 12.5 points on LiveMath, while replacing hundreds to thousands of Markdown skill tokens with a few virtual tokens. We further study agentic execution as a harder boundary case, where sparse trajectory imitation provides useful signal but does not yet robustly compress long-horizon procedural behavior. More broadly, the results suggest that some task skills are better treated not as additional Markdown to be reinterpreted at inference time, but as compact latent controls over how a frozen model enters the task.

</details>


### [189] [Automating SKILL.md Generation for Computer-Using Agents via Interaction Trajectory Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20363)
*Yuexing Hao,Xiaomin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出从GUI交互轨迹挖掘技能库的三阶段流程，虽能发现高纯度可解释技能簇，但无法有效提升下游策略性能，揭示了当前方法在跨域策略改进方面的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式技能库虽能提高计算机使用智能体的可解释性，但尚不清楚从交互数据挖掘的技能库是否能有效改进下游策略，本文旨在探究这一问题。

Method: 采用三阶段流程：(1)分割GUI轨迹；(2)将片段聚类为候选技能；(3)利用得到的标注训练技能感知策略。使用GRPO方法进行训练，并在InteraSkill Workflows和BrowseComp+基准上评估。

Result: 在源基准上聚类结果可读：8个聚类中有5个对InteraSkill Workflows标签的纯度至少为0.95。但可读性不意味着迁移性：GRPO仅将IW技能步骤准确率从18.5%提升至20.5%，对BrowseComp+基本无改善，在关键源域指标上甚至不如简单的频率先验。

Conclusion: 本研究为诊断性分析：轨迹挖掘可以揭示可检查的技能结构，但当前的边界检测器、无序片段表示和离线奖励模型不足以实现可靠的跨域策略改进，需要进一步研究。

Abstract: Explicit skill libraries make computer-using agents easier to inspect, but it remains unclear whether such libraries can be mined from interaction data in a way that improves downstream policies. We study this question through a three-stage pipeline that segments GUI trajectories, clusters segments into candidate skills, and trains a skill-aware policy from the resulting annotations. The mined clusters are readable on the source benchmark: five of eight clusters have at least 0.95 purity against InteraSkill Workflows labels. However, readability does not imply transfer. GRPO improves IW skill-step accuracy only from 18.5\% to 20.5\%, leaves BrowseComp+ essentially unchanged, and underperforms trivial frequency priors on key source-domain metrics. We therefore present the method as a diagnostic study: trajectory mining can expose inspectable skill structure, but the current boundary detector, orderless segment representation, and offline reward model are insufficient for reliable cross-domain policy improvement.

</details>


### [190] [Rethinking Shrinkage Bias in LLM FP4 Pretraining: Geometric Origin, Systemic Impact, and UFP4 Recipe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20381)
*Qian Zhao,Kunlong Chen,Changxin Tian,Zhonghui Jiang,Haitao Zhang,Chaofan Yu,Peijie Jiang,Mingliang Gong,Jia Liu,Ziqi Liu,Zhiqiang Zhang,Jun Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出UFP4方法，一种基于均匀格式的4位训练方案，解决了现有E2M1格式的收缩偏差问题，在大规模预训练中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前FP4训练硬件方案（如NVIDIA Blackwell/Rubin和AMD MI350系列）主要采用E2M1数据格式，但本文发现该非均匀格式存在"收缩偏差"这一根本局限性，即由于其可表示区间的几何不对称性导致的系统性负舍入误差，这会导致训练不稳定。

Method: 提出UFP4方法：采用均匀网格格式（E1M2/INT4），将随机Hadamard变换（RHT）应用于所有三个训练GEMM，同时将随机舍入仅限于dY，从而避免网格几何误差并获得更高质量量化。

Result: 在Dense 1.5B、MoE 7.9B和MoE 124B长周期预训练实验中，UFP4相比基于E2M1的强基线方法，始终实现了更低的BF16相对损失退化，并通过缩放定律分析和消融研究得到验证。

Conclusion: 研究结果建议未来的加速器应将E1M2/INT4风格的均匀4位网格作为与E2M1同等地位的一流训练原语加以支持，为硬件设计提供了重要指导。

Abstract: FP4 training promises substantial reductions in memory and computation cost for LLM pretraining, yet current FP4 hardware paths and recipes, including NVIDIA Blackwell/Rubin-class systems and AMD MI350-series GPUs, remain centered on E2M1 data elements. In this study, we identify a fundamental limitation of that choice: non-uniform formats such as E2M1 inherently suffer from Shrinkage Bias, a systematic negative rounding error caused by the geometric asymmetry of their representable bins. We show that this bias accumulates multiplicatively across layers and is amplified by the Random Hadamard Transform (RHT), providing a unified explanation for the training instability observed in existing E2M1-based FP4 recipes. In contrast, uniform grids (E1M2/INT4) bypass this grid-geometry error and better convert the improved bucket utilization from RHT into higher quantization quality. Based on this finding, we propose UFP4, a uniform 4-bit training recipe that applies RHT to all three training GEMMs while restricting stochastic rounding to dY alone. On Dense 1.5B, MoE 7.9B, and MoE 124B long-run pretraining, UFP4 consistently achieves lower BF16-relative loss degradation than strong E2M1-based baselines, supported by scaling-law analysis and ablation studies. Our results suggest that future accelerators should support E1M2/INT4-style uniform 4-bit grids as first-class training primitives alongside E2M1.

</details>


### [191] [Interpretable Sperm Morphology Classification via Attention-Guided Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20438)
*Zahra Asghari Varzaneh,Reza Khoshkangini,Thomas Ebner,Lars Johansson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种注意力引导的深度学习框架，用于精子形态分类，在公开数据集上实现了超过90%的准确率，并通过可视化技术增强了模型的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 男性不育是夫妻不育的主要原因，常与精子形态异常有关。虽然深度学习模型能够提供自动化分析，但大多数模型缺乏可解释性，限制了其在临床实践中的应用。

Method: 将预训练的EfficientNet-B0与卷积块注意力模块(CBAM)相结合，构建注意力引导的深度学习框架，使模型能够聚焦于精子头部的关键区域。同时采用Grad-CAM++可视化技术突出影响模型决策的特征。

Result: 在SMIDS和HuSHem公开数据集上，模型分别达到90.2%和93.9%的准确率(宏F1分数分别为0.913和0.948)，性能优于SimpleCNN和标准EfficientNet-B0模型。Grad-CAM++可视化成功展示了模型决策的关键特征。

Conclusion: 该框架兼具高准确性和良好的可解释性，是一个适用于生育诊所自动化精子分析的实用工具，有望促进深度学习技术在临床精子形态分析中的应用。

Abstract: Male infertility is a major cause of couple infertility, often linked to abnormal sperm morphology. While deep learning models offer automated analysis, most lack interpretability, limiting their clinical adoption. This study proposes an attention-guided deep learning framework for sperm morphology classification. We combine a pretrained EfficientNet-B0 with a Convolutional Block Attention Module (CBAM) to focus on key areas of the sperm head, improving both accuracy and interpretability. Evaluated on the SMIDS and HuSHem public datasets, our model achieves accuracies of 90.2% and 93.9% (macro F1 scores of 0.913 and 0.948), outperforming SimpleCNN and standard EfficientNet-B0. Furthermore, we use Grad-CAM++ visualizations to highlight features influencing the model's decisions. The results demonstrate that this accurate and transparent framework is a practical tool for automated sperm analysis in fertility clinics.

</details>


### [192] [Context-Aware Hierarchical Bayesian Modeling of IVF Laboratory Environmental Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20459)
*Zahra Asghari Varzaneh,Reza Khoshkangini,Pia Saldeen,Lars Johansson,Thomas Ebner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究证明利用高分辨率实验室环境数据可显著提升IVF妊娠率预测性能。通过构建55个上下文感知时间特征，预测误差降至1.27%（原始平均值为3-5%）。分层贝叶斯Beta回归模型在跨诊所验证中实现R²=0.86，对35-39岁年龄组预测误差降低64%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IVF妊娠率预测模型主要依赖患者层面变量，而高分辨率实验室环境数据未被充分利用。原始传感器平均值无法捕捉培养箱微环境的动态变化特征，这种数据利用方式存在不足，亟需开发更有效的环境特征工程方法。

Method: 构建55个上下文感知时间特征，包括滚动热稳定性、温湿度联合依从性、峰值应力持续时间和应力后恢复速度，以捕捉培养箱微环境动态。训练分层贝叶斯Beta回归模型，通过部分池化在亚洲和北欧诊所之间共享环境影响，同时保留各诊所特定基线。

Result: 在亚洲IVF诊所61周数据上，时间特征将交叉验证预测误差降至1.27%（原始平均值为3-5%）。在北欧诊所保留数据上，模型实现R²=0.86，对35-39岁年龄组相比朴素基线预测误差降低64%。

Conclusion: 结构化环境监测包含具有临床意义且可迁移的信号，可为IVF妊娠率预测提供重要价值。环境数据的有效利用和跨诊所知识迁移策略具有实际应用潜力。

Abstract: IVF pregnancy rates are routinely modeled using patient-level variables, while high-resolution laboratory environmental data remain underutilized. We show that this is a missed opportunity. Rather than relying on raw sensor averages, we engineer 55 context-aware temporal features, including rolling thermal stability, simultaneous temperature-humidity adherence, peak stress duration, and post-stress recovery speed, that capture the dynamics of incubator microenvironments. On 61 weeks of data from an Asian IVF clinic, these features reduce cross-validated prediction error to 1.27%, compared to 3-5% for raw averages. We then train a hierarchical Bayesian Beta regression model that shares environmental effects across an Asian and a Northern European clinic via partial pooling, while preserving site-specific baselines. On held-out data from the Northern European clinic, the model achieves R2 = 0.86 and a 64% error reduction for the 35-39 age group over a naive baseline, demonstrating that structured environmental monitoring contains clinically meaningful, transferable signal.

</details>


### [193] [What Do Safety-Aligned LLMs Learn From Mixed Compliance Demonstrations?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20508)
*Sihui Dai,Mann Patel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了语言模型如何解读不同类型的上下文合规演示，发现良性演示与有害演示不可互换，偏好优化是防止良性演示增加有害合规的关键训练阶段，演示排序存在强近因偏差，模型在拒绝与上下文学习的交互方式上存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究表明上下文演示可以对语言模型进行越狱攻击，但尚不清楚模型如何解读不同类型的合规演示，以及演示组合如何驱动有害合规行为。本文旨在深入表征基于演示的越狱攻击的工作机制。

Method: 将良性合规演示（无害请求+有帮助的响应）与有害合规演示（有害请求+有帮助的响应）混合，测试三种关于演示组合如何驱动有害合规的假设，并在四个模型上进行实验验证。

Result: 发现：(1)良性演示和有害演示不可互换，良性演示对有害合规的影响因模型而异（可能减少或增加）；(2)偏好优化是防止良性演示增加有害合规的关键训练阶段；(3)演示排序表现出强烈的近因偏差；(4)不同模型在拒绝与上下文学习的交互方式上存在差异——有些在拒绝时仍采用演示的格式，有些则在拒绝时覆盖所有上下文信号。

Conclusion: 本研究超越了简单地证明基于演示的越狱攻击有效，深入刻画了其工作机制：模型从合规演示中提取的内容取决于演示内容、排序和训练方法，这为理解和防御此类攻击提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Prior work has shown that in-context demonstrations can jailbreak language models, but it remains unclear how models interpret different types of compliance demonstrations. We study this by mixing benign compliance demonstrations (non-harmful request, helpful response) with harmful compliance demonstrations (harmful request, helpful response) and testing three hypotheses about how demonstration composition drives harmful compliance. Across four models, we find that benign and harmful demonstrations are not interchangeable: benign demonstrations can either reduce or increase harmful compliance depending on the model. We further show that preference optimization is the critical training stage that prevents benign demonstrations from increasing harmful compliance, that demonstration ordering exhibits strong recency bias, and that models differ in how refusal interacts with in-context learning: some adopt demonstrated formatting even when refusing, while others override all in-context signals upon refusal. Taken together, this work moves beyond showing that demonstration-based jailbreaking works to characterizing how it works: what models extract from compliance demonstrations depends on demonstration content, ordering, and training methodology.

</details>


### [194] [Multi-LCB: Extending LiveCodeBench to Multiple Programming Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20517)
*Maria Ivanova,Pavel Zadorozhny,Rodion Levichev,Ivan Petrov,Adamenko Pavel,Ivan Lopatin,Alexey Kutalev,Dmitrii Babaev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Multi-LCB，一个扩展LiveCodeBench的多编程语言代码生成评估基准，支持12种编程语言，揭示了当前大语言模型在多语言编程能力上的显著差距和Python过拟合问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LiveCodeBench基准仅限于Python语言评估，无法反映大语言模型在真实软件工程中跨多种编程语言的泛化能力，留下了评估模型多语言代码生成能力的空白。

Method: 构建Multi-LCB基准，将LiveCodeBench的Python任务转换为其他11种编程语言的等效任务，保持原有的污染控制和评估协议，完全兼容原始LCB格式，可自动跟踪未来LCB更新。

Result: 对24个LLM进行指令和推理评估，发现Python过拟合证据、语言特定污染现象，以及多语言性能的显著差异，揭示了当前模型在跨语言代码生成能力上的关键缺陷。

Conclusion: Multi-LCB作为一个严格的多编程语言代码评估新基准，直接解决了LCB的主要局限性，暴露了当前LLM能力的关键差距，为评估模型的真实代码生成能力提供了更全面的视角。

Abstract: LiveCodeBench (LCB) has recently become a widely adopted benchmark for evaluating large language models (LLMs) on code-generation tasks. By curating competitive programming problems, constantly adding fresh problems to the set, and filtering them by release dates, LCB provides contamination-aware evaluation and offers a holistic view of coding capability. However, LCB remains restricted to Python, leaving open the question of whether LLMs can generalize across the diverse programming languages required in real-world software engineering.
  We introduce Multi-LCB, a benchmark for evaluating LLMs across twelve programming languages, including Python. Multi-LCB transforms Python tasks from the LCB dataset into equivalent tasks in other languages while preserving LCB's contamination controls and evaluation protocol. Because it is fully compatible with the original LCB format, Multi-LCB will automatically track future LCB updates, enabling systematic assessment of cross-language code generation competence and requiring models to sustain performance well beyond Python.
  We evaluated 24 LLMs for instruction and reasoning on Multi-LCB, uncovering evidence of Python overfitting, language-specific contamination, and substantial disparities in multilingual performance. Our results establish Multi-LCB as a rigorous new benchmark for multi-programming-language code evaluation, directly addressing LCB's primary limitation and exposing critical gaps in current LLM capabilities.

</details>


### [195] [FlowEdit: Associative Memory for Lifelong Pronunciation Adaptation in Flow-Matching TTS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20518)
*Harshit Singh,Ayush Pratap Singh,Nityanand Mathur*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了FlowEdit，一种针对冻结流匹配TTS模型的终身适应框架，通过学习潜在条件编辑而非权重更新来纠正发音错误，在多语言专有名词基准测试中将目标词音素错误率降低了92.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的流匹配文本转语音系统虽然在零样本质量上表现优异，但在部署后保持静态：对于词汇表外的专有名词发音错误会持续存在，除非对模型进行重新训练。

Method: FlowEdit优化文本嵌入空间中的标记级扰动，将发音纠正作为潜在条件编辑学习，并将纠正存储在现代Hopfield网络中作为内容可寻址的情景记忆；在推理时通过带有相似性门的软注意力机制检索纠正，实现模糊形态匹配。

Result: 在包含18个语系312个多语言专有名词的基准测试中，FlowEdit相对于零样本基线将目标词音素错误率降低了92.7%，同时保持相同的通用语音质量；纠正过程在单个GPU上约15秒完成。

Conclusion: FlowEdit成功实现了冻结流匹配TTS模型的终身适应，无需重新训练即可有效纠正专有名词发音错误，且不损害通用语音质量，为TTS系统的部署后优化提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Flow-matching text-to-speech systems achieve remarkable zero-shot quality but remain static after deployment: pronunciation errors on out-of-vocabulary proper nouns persist unless the model is retrained. We introduce FlowEdit, a life-long adaptation framework for frozen flow-matching TTS that learns pronunciation corrections as latent conditioning edits rather than weight updates. When corrective feedback is provided, FlowEdit optimizes a token-level perturbation in the text embedding space, then stores the correction in a Modern Hopfield Network serving as content-addressable episodic memory. At inference, corrections are retrieved via soft attention with a similarity gate, enabling fuzzy morphological matching. On our curated benchmark of 312 multilingual proper nouns across 18 language families, FlowEdit reduces target-word Phoneme Error Rate by 92.7% relative to the zero-shot baseline while maintaining identical general-speech quality. Corrections complete in approximately 15 seconds on a single GPU.

</details>


### [196] [DeepSWIP: Quotient-WMC Counterfactuals for Neural Probabilistic Logic Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20526)
*Saimun Habib,Vaishak Belle,Fengxiang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了DeepSWIP，一种针对DeepProbLog程序的单世界反事实语义方法，通过神经物化将固定上下文的神经谓词转换为ProbLog选择，并利用SWIPs在单个转换程序上通过加权模型计数（WMC）计算反事实，避免了传统方法的内源复制，实现了精确推断和2.14倍的速度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经符号系统（如DeepProbLog）结合了神经感知和概率逻辑，但标准推断仅支持关联推理。反事实推理需要因果语义来处理干预和证据，这在现有系统中是缺失的。

Method: 引入DeepSWIP单世界反事实语义，通过神经物化将固定上下文的神经谓词归约为普通ProbLog选择；应用单世界干预程序（SWIPs）；在单个转换程序上通过加权模型计数（WMC）计算反事实。在有限奠基和唯一支持模型假设下，相对于学习到的物化FCM，DeepSWIP是精确的。

Result: 在MPI3D数据集上针对12,000个查询的实验验证了转换的正确性（与DeepTwin构建对比）；相比DeepTwin方法获得2.14倍的推断加速（避免了内源复制）；SUMO HOV实验表明神经校准退化会偏置插件估计，而正确范围的随机策略AIPW估计器可消除大部分一阶偏差。

Conclusion: DeepSWIP成功将单世界反事实语义引入神经符号系统，提供了精确且高效的反事实推断方法；标准ProbLog条件商WMC形式能够识别活跃神经概率并解释干预清理、校准敏感性和罕见证据不稳定性现象。

Abstract: Neurosymbolic systems such as DeepProbLog combine neural perception with probabilistic logic, but standard inference is associational. Counterfactual reasoning additionally requires a causal semantics for interventions and evidence. We introduce DeepSWIP, a single-world counterfactual semantics for DeepProbLog programs. Using neural materialization, we reduce fixed-context neural predicates to ordinary ProbLog choices, apply Single World Intervention Programs (SWIPs), and compute counterfactuals by weighted model counting (WMC) over a single transformed program. Under finite grounding and unique-supported-model assumptions, DeepSWIP is exact relative to the learned materialized FCM. The standard quotient-WMC form of ProbLog conditionals identifies active neural probabilities and explains intervention cleaning, calibration sensitivity, and rare-evidence instability. Experiments on MPI3D confirm the transformation against a DeepTwin construction against 12,000 queries, as predicted and a 2.14$\times$ inference speedup from avoiding the Twin's endogenous duplication. A SUMO HOV experiment shows that neural calibration degradation biases plug-in estimates, while a correctly scoped randomized-policy AIPW estimator removes most first-order bias for population mean and ATE estimands. Code is at https://github.com/saibib/deep_SWIP.

</details>


### [197] [LedgerAgent: Structured State for Policy-Adherent Tool-Calling Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20529)
*Md Nayem Uddin,Amir Saeidi,Eduardo Blanco,Chitta Baral*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LedgerAgent方法，通过独立账本维护任务状态并检查策略约束，有效解决了工具调用代理中的状态管理隐式化导致的策略违规问题，在客户服务领域显著提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的工具调用代理采用隐式状态管理，将观察、工具返回和策略指令直接放入提示中，导致代理需要每次从提示中重建相关状态。这种设计存在两个常见失败模式：(1)代理可能检索到正确事实，但后续决策基于过时、缺失或错误的信息；(2)语法有效的工具调用仍可能违反依赖当前任务状态的领域策略。

Method: 引入LedgerAgent推理时方法：维护一个独立账本(large ledger)来显式记录观察到的任务状态（包括相关事实、标识符、约束和条件），并将状态渲染到提示中；在执行改变环境的工具调用前，利用账本检查依赖状态的策略约束，阻止策略违规。

Result: 在四个客户服务领域和包含开源及闭源模型的混合面板上测试，LedgerAgent在平均pass^k指标上优于标准基于提示的工具调用方法，在更严格的多试验一致性指标下获得最大增益。

Conclusion: 通过显式状态管理和策略约束检查，LedgerAgent有效提升了策略遵循型工具调用代理的性能，为客服领域的代理设计提供了更可靠的状态管理方案。

Abstract: Policy-adherent tool-calling agents in customer-service domains must maintain task states across turns while calling tools and obeying domain policies. Task states consist of relevant facts, identifiers, constraints, and conditions observed through user interaction and tool calls. In standard agents, task states are not represented separately. Observations, tool returns, and policy instructions are placed in the prompt, leaving agents to reconstruct the relevant states from the prompt each time they decide what to do next. This design makes state management implicit, creating two common failure modes. An agent may retrieve the right facts but later ground its decision in stale, missing, or incorrect information; and a syntactically valid tool call may still violate a domain policy that depends on the current task state. We introduce \textsc{LedgerAgent}, an inference-time method for tool-calling agents that maintains observed task states in a separate ledger and renders the states into the prompt. The ledger is also used to check state-dependent policy constraints before environment-changing tool calls are executed, blocking policy violations. Across four customer-service domains and a mixed panel of open- and closed-weight models, \textsc{LedgerAgent} improves average pass\textasciicircum{}k over a standard prompt-based tool-calling approach, with the largest gains under stricter multi-trial consistency metrics.

</details>


### [198] [How Do Instructions Shape Speech? Cross-Attention Attribution for Style-Captioned Text-to-Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20532)
*Nityanand Mathur,Hamees Sayed,Wasim Madha,Apoorv Singh,Sameer Khurana,Akshat Mandloi,Sudarshan Kamath*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 首次将DAAM框架适配到语音扩散模型，通过跨注意力归因分析揭示风格描述文本如何影响语音生成，发现风格token具有全局调控作用且在第17层达到最大选择性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风格描述文本到语音系统使用自然语言控制语音特征，但单个词汇如何影响声学输出仍不清楚。理解这一机制对于诊断失败模式和提升表现力TTS的可控性至关重要。

Method: 提出语音扩散模型的跨注意力归因方法，首次将DAAM框架适配到语音领域，应用于CapSpeech-TTS系统。该方法提取25层和24个ODE步骤的逐token热力图，分析了3,600个（风格描述，文本转录）组合，包括120个风格描述各自调节30个文本转录的生成。

Result: （1）风格token的时间方差低于内容/功能token，证实了全局调节；（2）风格注意力与F0和能量相关；（3）风格调节在早期步骤和深层达到峰值；（4）注意力熵在第17层达到最小值，与风格重要性峰值同时出现，表明网络在最关键的风格阶段具有最大选择性。

Conclusion: 这是首个研究自然语言如何影响语音扩散模型跨注意力的工作，为理解风格描述文本的调控机制提供了重要见解，有助于改进TTS系统的可控性和诊断失败模式。

Abstract: Style-captioned text-to-speech systems use natural language to control voice characteristics, but how individual words influence acoustic output remains unclear. Understanding this is critical for diagnosing failure modes and improving controllability in expressive TTS. We propose cross-attention attribution for speech diffusion models, adapting the DAAM framework to the speech domain for the first time, and apply it to CapSpeech-TTS. Our method extracts per-token heatmaps across 25 layers and 24 ODE steps. We analyze 3,600 (style caption, text transcript) combinations comprising 120 style captions conditioning the generation of 30 text transcripts each, revealing how caption tokens shape waveforms. Results show: (1) style tokens have lower temporal variance than content/function tokens, confirming global conditioning; (2) style attention correlates with F0 and energy; (3) style conditioning peaks in early steps and deep layers; (4) attention entropy reaches its minimum at layer 17, co-occurring with the style importance peak, indicating maximal network selectivity at the most style-critical stage. This is the first study of how natural language influences cross-attention in speech diffusion models

</details>


### [199] [Toward Calibrated Mixture-of-Experts Under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20544)
*Gina Wong,Drew Prinster,Suchi Saria,Rama Chellappa,Anqi Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了混合专家(MoE)模型在分布偏移下的校准行为，发现专家级校准对硬路由模型充分但对软路由模型不足，并提出对抗重加权方法来改善精度-校准权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明在个体预测器层面强制校准可以提升集成模型的准确性和校准性，混合专家模型尤其表现出显著的经验改进，但校准在何种条件下有助于MoE模型尚未被充分理解，特别是在分布偏移场景下的行为机制不明。

Method: 分析路由机制与专家级校准的交互作用，证明专家校准在硬路由模型中能确保整体模型校准但对软路由模型不足；提出对抗重加权方法，惩罚分布偏移下路由聚合的校准误差。

Result: 专家校准足以在广泛分布偏移下确保硬路由模型的校准，但对软路由模型不足；提出的对抗重加权方法在多种模型类别、预测任务和分布偏移下均改善了精度-校准权衡，在平均表现和困难数据子集上均有提升。

Conclusion: 路由机制类型决定了专家校准对整体模型校准的充分性；通过对抗重加权方法可以有效解决软路由模型在分布偏移下的校准问题，提升模型可靠性。

Abstract: Calibration aligns a model's predictive uncertainty with the frequencies of its empirical outcomes and is important for understanding and trusting reported probabilities. Recent work shows that enforcing calibration at the level of individual predictors can improve ensemble accuracy and calibration, with mixture-of-experts (MoE) models showing strong empirical improvements in particular; however, the conditions under which calibration helps MoE are not well understood. In this work, we study how MoE models behave under distribution shift, focusing on how routing mechanisms interact with expert-level calibration. We show that expert calibration is sufficient to ensure calibration of the overall model under a broad class of distribution shifts in hard-routed models, but is insufficient for calibrating soft-routed models. To address this, we propose an adversarial reweighting that penalizes calibration errors of the routed aggregate under distribution shift, and we demonstrate that it improves the accuracy-calibration tradeoff both on average and on difficult subsets of the data, across model classes, prediction tasks, and distribution shifts.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [200] [Certified Euclidean-Residue Minimal-Alignment Switch Decompositions for Three Edge-Disjoint Hamiltonian Cycles in Eisenstein--Jacobi Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19832)
*Bader Albader*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对非互质EJ网络，提出了一种基于局部交换演算的三边不相交哈密顿圈分解方法，通过d-1次组件间交换构造前两个哈密顿圈，第三个作为剩余边补集获得，实现了最小交换次数分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EJ网络是六度商格互连网络。当生成器参数d=1时，三个自然单位方向直接给出三条边不相交哈密顿圈；当d>1时，每个单位方向分裂成d个圈，EDHC问题转化为圈拼接问题。现有方法依赖矩形表示和交换调度证明存在性，但缺乏紧凑的公式化方法。

Method: 在自然Cayley几何中开发了局部交换演算。通过最小可能的d-1次组件间交换构造前两个哈密顿圈，第三个因子作为未使用边的补集获得。证明包含四个要素：组件标签坍缩、锚点消除、提升交换代表的非冲突性，以及连通补集关联性。

Result: 获得了紧凑的、公式驱动的最小交换分解方案，适用于欧几里得残差族，并通过符号化方式证明了补集关联性。该方法仅需d-1次交换即可构造每个哈密顿圈，达到理论最小值。

Conclusion: 本文贡献不是对所有非互质EJ网络的新存在性定理，而是针对可写出代数补集关联证书的参数范围，提供了公式化的最小交换分解方法。所有无限族定理均通过代数证明完成，不依赖有限见证或计算枚举，数据表仅用于验证和复现公式。

Abstract: Eisenstein--Jacobi (EJ) networks are degree-six quotient-lattice interconnection networks. For a generator $α=a+bρ$, let $N=a^2+ab+b^2$ and $d=\gcd(a,b)$. If $d=1$, the three natural unit directions already give three edge-disjoint Hamiltonian cycles. If $d>1$, each unit direction splits into $d$ cycles and the EDHC problem becomes a cycle-splicing problem. Existing non-coprime EJ decompositions prove existence by using a rectangular representation and exchange schedules. This paper develops a different, local switch calculus in the natural Cayley geometry. The first two Hamiltonian cycles are built using the minimum possible $d-1$ intercomponent switches each, and the third factor is obtained as the unused edge complement. The contribution is deliberately not a new existence theorem for all non-coprime EJ networks; rather, it is a compact, formula-driven, minimal-switch decomposition for Euclidean-residue families whose complement incidence is proved symbolically. The proof separates four ingredients: component-label collapse, anchor cancellation, noncollision of lifted switch representatives, and connected complement incidence. No infinite-family theorem in this manuscript is proved by finite witnesses or by computational enumeration. The theorem scope is stated for the parameter ranges where an algebraic complement-incidence certificate is written down. Tables and CSV data are used only to verify and reproduce the formulas, never as proof of an infinite-family theorem.

</details>


### [201] [A Topos-Theoretic Interpretation of Blockchain Systems: Sheaves of Consensus and the Logic of Decentralized Truth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19519)
*Manuel Hernández,Eduardo Sánchez-Soto*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出使用拓扑斯理论作为区块链系统的原生数学建模语言，以克服传统有限状态机模型将共识机制排除在形式模型之外的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有区块链形式化模型（主要是有限状态机或标记转换系统）将区块链最核心、最困难的特性——去中心化环境下的共识达成——视为复杂的实现细节，排斥在形式模型本身之外。但共识过程并非辅助特性，而是区块链计算现象的本质所在。

Method: 提出以拓扑斯理论（层范畴理论）作为新的数学语言来构建区块链系统的形式化模型。该理论能够自然地描述由局部一致性定义的系统以及全局真理的构建过程。

Result: 论证了拓扑斯理论是区块链系统的原生数学语言，能够忠实地建模共识过程，将去中心化环境下的局部一致性与全局真理构建统一纳入形式化框架中。

Conclusion: 区块链系统需要一个能够内在表达共识机制的新型数学语言，拓扑斯理论提供了这样的语言基础，使得共识不再作为外部实现细节，而是形式模型的核心组成部分。

Abstract: The predominant formal models for blockchain systems, particularly smart contracts, have largely been drawn from the classical theory of computation, with the finite state machine (FSM) or labeled transition system serving as the primary conceptual tool. However, the FSM relegates the most difficult and novel aspect of a blockchain -- the achievement of consensus in a decentralized environment -- to a complex, often messy, implementation detail that lies outside the formal model itself. But the process of consensus is not an ancillary feature; it is the very essence of the computational phenomenon. To model it faithfully, a new mathematical language is required. The central thesis of this work is that topos theory, the theory of categories of sheaves, provides the native mathematical language for systems defined by local consistency and the construction of global truth.

</details>


### [202] [Fault-Tolerant Shared-Relay Communication in Circulant Interconnection Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19833)
*Bader Albader,Galal Hassan,Mohamed R. Al-Mulla*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了有向循环网络中双跳原语的容错能力，提出参数$R(n,m)$量化最坏情况下的共享中继数量，并开发了相应的网络设计框架。实验表明优化设计的生成器选择能在接近理论下界(约1.16-1.63倍)内实现容错，而标准区间生成器即使度数更大也可能结构性地失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环互连网络具有对称寻址、紧凑生成器描述和均匀局部连接性等优势。然而，在给定节点数$n$和度数预算$m$的约束下，如何量化并优化最坏情况下的共享中继复用度$R(n,m)$以保证容错能力，是一个关键的网络设计问题。

Method: 1. 引入共享中继概念：若某节点到有序终端对的两个节点都有出链，则该节点为其共享中继；
2. 利用循环差分-复用条件作为数学工具建立可行性分析；
3. 提出核心参数$R(n,m)$和$D_f(n)$；
4. 针对区间循环网络给出否定定理；
5. 设计中继表预处理和查找算法；
6. 提供对抗性和随机故障保证；
7. 对526,539个生成器集进行可复现研究。

Result: 生成器选择对最坏情况下中继生存能力具有决定性影响：优化的阈值设计能在计数下界的约1.16-1.63倍范围内实现$f$-中继容错；而标准区间生成器即使度数大得多也可能结构性失效；完成了小$n$值的精确校准；软件微基准测试验证了查找与搜索的性能差异。

Conclusion: 有向循环网络的容错能力严重依赖于生成器的选择。本文提出的网络设计框架为优化容错能力提供了理论基础和实践工具，证明优化设计可以高效实现容错目标，而传统标准方法可能存在结构性缺陷。

Abstract: Circulant interconnection networks provide symmetric addressing, compact generator descriptions, and uniform local connectivity. This paper maps a degree--redundancy landscape for a fault-tolerant two-hop primitive in directed circulants: given $n$ nodes and degree budget $m$, how large can the worst-case shared-relay multiplicity $R(n,m)$ be? A node is a shared relay for an ordered terminal pair if it has outgoing links to both terminals; an $f$-relay-fault-tolerant circulant requires at least $f+1$ such relays for every pair. The underlying feasibility condition is a cyclic difference-multiplicity condition, which we use as a mathematical tool rather than claim as a new object. The contribution is the network-design framework around this tool: the parameters $R(n,m)$ and $D_f(n)$, a negative theorem for interval circulants, relay-table preprocessing and lookup algorithms, adversarial and random failure guarantees, load-balance scope, certified upper-bound interpretation of heuristic designs, exact small-$n$ calibration, a software lookup-versus-search microbenchmark, and a reproducible study of 526,539 generator sets. The results show that generator choice critically determines worst-case relay survivability: optimized threshold designs achieve $f$-relay-fault tolerance within about $1.16$--$1.63$ of the counting lower bound, while standard interval generators can fail structurally even at much larger degrees.

</details>


### [203] [The Sheaf Laplacian: A Topological Framework for Data Fusion and Consensus in Distributed Sensing Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19529)
*Manuel Hernández,Eduardo Sánchez-Soto*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文指出传统简单图网络模型不足以描述现代分布式系统的复杂性，提出利用层论作为新的数学语言，并证明层拉普拉斯算子可用于分布式传感网络的数据融合与共识建立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代分布式系统具有异构代理、高维多模态数据流及复杂上下文依赖关系等特征，传统基于简单图的数学模型在表达能力上存在根本性不足。

Method: 引入层论这一数学工具，构建具有更强表达能力的网络描述语言，并利用层拉普拉斯算子进行数据处理。

Result: 证明了层拉普拉斯算子是分布式传感网络中实现数据融合和建立共识的合适机制。

Conclusion: 层论为描述现代分布式系统提供了必要的数学语言，能够克服传统网络模型的局限性。

Abstract: We argue here that traditional network models, which are overwhelmingly based on the mathematical construct of a simple graph, are fundamentally insufficient for capturing the complexity of modern distributed systems. Such systems are characterized by heterogeneous agents with diverse capabilities, high-dimensional and multi-modal data streams, and intricate, context-dependent relationships that cannot be adequately described by a simple connection or a scalar weight. The limitations of these classical models necessitate a new mathematical language, one with far greater expressive power. We have found that sheaf theory provides us with such a language. Moreover, we show that the sheaf Laplacian is a suitable mechanism for data fusion and establishing consensus within distributed sensing networks.

</details>


### [204] [Multi-Orientation Edge-Minimum Repair for Non-Redundant Fault-Tolerant Broadcasting in Dense Eisenstein--Jacobi Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19834)
*Bader Albader*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了EJ-MOEM方法，实现了密集EJ网络中的非冗余一对多广播容错修复，证明了修复边数量的充要性及深度上界定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集Eisenstein-Jacobi网络是一种六度代数互连网络，需要研究其在节点故障情况下的容错广播修复机制，确保消息能够准确传递给所有健康节点且不使用故障节点。

Method: 提出EJ-MOEM（多方向边最小修复）方法：评估常数规模的六边形广播树方向族，选择故障感知候选，将故障修剪树收缩为健康组件，使用外部组件交叉修复边重新连接这些组件，构建健康子图的根生成树。采用三带表示、扇区后缀附加引理、非相邻扇区分离引理和六方向屏蔽分类技术。

Result: 证明对于选定方向（其故障修剪组件图连通），恰好c-1条外部修复边是必要且充分的（c为健康组件数）；证明深度证书定理：每个单故障放置存在深度至多t+1的修复，每个双故障放置存在深度至多t+2的修复。实验验证覆盖t=2至30及大尺度随机测试（至t=200），包括t=18时525,825种双故障放置，成功率100%。

Conclusion: EJ-MOEM方法成功实现了密集EJ网络中非冗余容错广播，保证每个健康节点恰好接收一次消息，保留原始健康树组件，理论界限紧致且经过广泛验证。

Abstract: Dense Eisenstein--Jacobi (EJ) networks are degree-six algebraic interconnection networks whose finite quotient geometry is naturally represented by a hexagonal axial-coordinate ball. This paper studies non-redundant one-to-all broadcast repair in the dense EJ network generated by $α=(t+1)+tω$, where $t$ is the network diameter. We propose EJ-MOEM, a multi-orientation edge-minimum repair method that evaluates a constant-size family of hexagonal broadcast-tree orientations, selects a fault-aware candidate, contracts the fault-pruned tree into healthy components, and reconnects these components using external component-crossing repair edges. The resulting structure is a rooted spanning tree of the healthy subgraph: every healthy node receives the message exactly once, no faulty node is used, and the original healthy tree components are preserved. We prove that, for a chosen orientation whose fault-pruned component graph is connected, exactly $c-1$ external repair edges are necessary and sufficient, where $c$ is the number of healthy components. We also prove a depth-certificate theorem for EJ coordinate-reduction trees: every one-fault placement admits a repair of depth at most $t+1$, and every two-fault placement admits a repair of depth at most $t+2$. The proof uses the three-strip representation of EJ hexagons, a sector-suffix attachment lemma, a non-adjacent-sector separation lemma, and a six-direction shielding classification for paired cuts. Extended validation includes exhaustive one- and two-fault enumeration for $t=2,\ldots,12,14,16,18$ (up to $N=1027$ and 525,825 two-fault placements at $t=18$), structured theorem-critical tests through $t=30$, and large random tests through $t=200$, all with 100\% success and no violation of the theorem.

</details>


### [205] [SAC: Disaggregated KV Cache System for Sparse Attention LLMs with CXL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19746)
*Ruiyang Ma,Teng Ma,Junru Li,Hantian Zha,Xuchun Shang,Qingda Hu,Zheng Liu,Xinjun Yang,Tao Ma,Guojie Luo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: SAC是首个针对稀疏注意力模型的 disaggregated KV cache 系统，利用CXL技术实现细粒度按需获取，相比传统RDMA方案在吞吐量上提升2.1倍，TTFT降低9.7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM向长上下文推理发展，内存容量成为主要瓶颈。现有基于RDMA的 disaggregated memory 方案为稀疏注意力模型fetch整个prefix KV cache，但稀疏注意力在解码时仅使用少量KV条目，导致严重的传输瓶颈和本地内存浪费。

Method: 提出SAC系统，利用Compute Express Link (CXL)的低延迟、缓存行粒度的load/store语义，在推理过程中按需仅获取所需的top-k KV条目，而非预先获取整个KV cache。

Result: 在DeepSeek-V3.2上使用SGLang进行评估，SAC相比RDMA基线实现了2.1倍更高的吞吐量、9.7倍更低的TTFT（Time To First Token）、以及1.8倍更低的TBT（Time Between Tokens）。

Conclusion: 基于CXL的 disaggregation 为新兴的稀疏注意力模型提供了更优的基础设施，证明了细粒度按需获取策略在稀疏注意力场景下显著优于传统的粗粒度预取方案。

Abstract: The scaling of LLMs toward long-context inference has shifted the primary serving system bottleneck from computation to memory capacity. Traditional solutions for dense attention models rely on RDMA-based disaggregated memory pools, which perform coarse-grained fetching of the entire prefix KV cache from remote storage to local memory before decoding. However, this approach is fundamentally inefficient for emerging sparse attention models. While only a small fraction of KV entries are active during decoding, these systems still fetch the full KV cache locally, leading to severe transmission bottlenecks and local memory wastage. To address this, we propose SAC, the first efficient disaggregated KV cache system optimized for sparse attention models. By leveraging the low-latency, cache-line granularity load/store semantics of Compute Express Link (CXL), SAC fetches only the required top-k KV entries on demand during inference. Evaluations on DeepSeek-V3.2 using SGLang show that SAC achieves 2.1x higher throughput, 9.7x lower TTFT, and 1.8x lower TBT compared to RDMA-based baselines, establishing CXL-based disaggregation as the superior infrastructure for emerging sparse attention models.

</details>


### [206] [EVM Workloads in the Wild: Evidence for Multi-Dimensional Gas Metering, State Growth, Delayed Execution, and Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19869)
*Lioba Heimbach,Kushal Babel,Jason Milionis*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文通过测量研究发现，EVM兼容区块链上的Gas计量假设（资源组合稳定、状态漂移不影响执行结果）在实际中不成立。Base和Ethereum的资源使用模式差异显著，执行结果对状态高度敏感，46.0%的Base交易和13.9%的Ethereum交易在不同历史状态下的Gas估算存在差异，这为多维Gas计量和状态增长定价提供了实证基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EVM兼容区块链的Gas计量机制基于两个假设：资源组合足够稳定以支持单一标量定价，以及交易提交到执行期间的状态漂移不会显著影响执行结果。本文旨在实证测量这些假设的失效程度，揭示当前Gas定价机制的局限性。

Method: 研究在2025年全年对Ethereum L1和Base L2进行区块级采样（每链每日3000个区块），将交易分解为操作码级执行Gas、内在Gas、退款和持久化状态增量。通过在历史状态上重执行2025年9月的交易，测量Gas使用和存储访问模式的状态敏感性变化。

Result: 资源组合极不稳定：Base上存储读取和计算分别占执行Gas的29.2%和24.3%，Ethereum则将34.9%用于存储写入。Ethereum在2025年Gas限制翻倍后向计算密集型转变，呈现类似Base的模式。Base的冷存储读取比例更高（49.7% vs Ethereum的39.6%）。持久化状态增长在Base达456GB（Ethereum仅38GB）。执行结果不稳定：46.0%的Base交易和13.9%的Ethereum交易在相近历史状态间Gas估算存在差异，MEV和DeFi活动敏感性最高。存储访问模式随状态变化而分化，影响访问列表有效性和并行执行。

Conclusion: 研究为多维Gas计量和显式状态增长定价提供了实证基础，揭示状态敏感的执行行为使工作负载估计复杂化，直接影响交易可预测性和用户体验。当前单维Gas定价机制已无法适应实际工作负载特征，需要重新设计定价模型。

Abstract: Gas metering on EVM-compatible blockchains assumes that execution conditions are stable: that the resource mix is constant enough to justify collapsing execution costs into a single scalar with fixed relative prices, and that state drift between submission and execution does not materially alter a transaction's outcome. We measure the extent to which this assumption fails.
  We present a trace-level measurement study of EVM workloads on Ethereum (L1) and Base (L2) throughout 2025, sampling 3,000 blocks per day per chain. We decompose each transaction into opcode-level execution gas, intrinsic gas, refunds, and persistent state deltas. To measure state sensitivity, we re-execute transactions from September 2025 on older states and record how gas usage and storage access patterns change.
  We find the resource mix to be far from stable: on Base, storage reads and compute account for 29.2% and 24.3% of execution gas, while Ethereum devotes 34.9% to storage writes. Ethereum's gas limit doubling during 2025 shifted its own profile toward compute-heavier, Base-like patterns. Base also exhibits a higher fraction of cold storage reads (49.7% versus 39.6% on Ethereum). Persistent state growth, a permanent cost priced as a transient one, reaches 456 GB on Base versus 38 GB on Ethereum.
  Execution outcomes are equally unstable: gas estimates vary across nearby historical states for 46.0% of transactions on Base, compared to 13.9% on Ethereum, with especially high sensitivity for MEV and DeFi activity. Storage access patterns also diverge across states, limiting the effectiveness of access lists and complicating parallel execution.
  Our work provides an empirical foundation for multi-dimensional gas metering and explicit pricing of state growth. They show that state-sensitive execution behavior complicates workload estimation, directly affecting transaction predictability and user experience.

</details>


### [207] [Online Dynamic Batching with Formal Guarantees for LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19989)
*Dian Li,Zekun Wang,Yaoru Wang,Jiahong Yan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了在线动态批处理(ODB)系统，解决了现代LLM训练中离线批处理采样器的核心假设问题，在保持训练质量的同时显著提升了数据吞吐量(最高4.43倍)，且无需模型、优化器或注意力内核的修改。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM训练打破了离线批处理采样器的核心假设：样本的真实训练成本只有在预处理、增强、模板化、分词和多模态视觉令牌扩展之后才能观察到。除非付出预处理和增强相关的长度缓存代价，否则批构建对决定填充、内存使用和GPU饱和度的数量是盲目的。

Method: 引入在线动态批处理(ODB)系统，这是一个DataLoader端的即插即用系统，将批形成移动到准确可观察性的点，同时保持DDP步对齐。形式化了分布式组对齐问题(DGAP)，并证明了在默认连接模式下具有无死锁有界终止性和可选非连接样本配额闭包。ODB不需要模型、优化器或注意力内核的更改。

Result: 在公共2B/8B Qwen3-VL运行中，相比固定批处理Standard，ODB在单节点Full FT/LoRA上提升字面发射样本吞吐量1.58-2.51倍，在两节点Full FT上提升1.71-3.78倍，质量与Standard相当；生产MM-Mix达到4.43倍。对比GMT/BMT离线令牌预算预言机，ODB在UltraChat/LLaVA上相差15%以内，在高CV ShareGPT4o上更快：单节点Full FT/LoRA为2.24-2.39倍，两节点Full FT为3.06-3.69倍。

Conclusion: ODB占据了高异构性LLM微调的在线/即插即用领域：在Standard相当质量下实现大吞吐量增益，具有形式化DGAP保证，无需长度缓存预计算或内核重写。为高异构性LLM微调提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Modern LLM training breaks a core assumption behind offline batch samplers: the true training cost of a sample is only observable after preprocessing, augmentation, templating, tokenization, and multimodal visual-token expansion. Unless one pays for a preprocessing- and augmentation-dependent length cache, batch construction is therefore blind to the quantity that determines padding, memory use, and GPU saturation. We introduce Online Dynamic Batching (ODB), a DataLoader-side drop-in system that moves batch formation to this point of accurate observability while preserving DDP step alignment. We formalize this synchronization requirement as the Distributed Group Alignment Problem and prove deadlock-free bounded termination with default join-mode identity coverage and opt-in non-join sample-quota closure. ODB requires no model, optimizer, or attention-kernel changes and is released as online-dynamic-batching with lightweight trainer adapters. Across public 2B/8B Qwen3-VL runs on UltraChat/LLaVA/ShareGPT4o, ODB improves literal emitted-sample throughput vs. fixed-batch Standard by 1.58-2.51x on single-node Full FT/LoRA and 1.71-3.78x on two-node Full FT, with Standard-comparable quality; production MM-Mix reaches 4.43x. Against GMT/BMT offline token-budget oracles, ODB is within 15% on UltraChat/LLaVA and faster on high-CV ShareGPT4o: 2.24-2.39x single-node Full FT/LoRA and 3.06-3.69x two-node Full FT. Together, ODB occupies the online/drop-in regime for high-heterogeneity LLM fine-tuning: large throughput gains at Standard-comparable quality, formal DGAP guarantees, and no length-cache precompute or kernel rewrites.

</details>


### [208] [ARGUS: Production-Scale Tracing and Performance Diagnosis for over 10,000-GPU Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20374)
*Jiasheng Zhou,Longbin Zeng,Clavis Chen,Ruiming Lu,Qinwei Yang,Leyi Ye,Ray Ying,Key Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ARGUS是一个面向万卡级GPU集群的低开销、细粒度、全时在线的训练可观测性系统，在保持低于2%开销的同时实现约3700倍的数据压缩，能够自动诊断多种性能异常。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模LLM训练需要全时在线、细粒度的可观测性来有效进行性能诊断。然而，粗粒度资源监控无法定位根因，而细粒度分析器会带来5%-30%的过高开销和海量追踪数据，使其在大规模生产集群中难以实现全时部署。

Method: ARGUS提出了一种分层观测方法：1）沿训练调用层次分解观测，包括CPU调用栈、框架语义和GPU内核执行；2）构建统一数据管道，实现约3700倍的数据压缩（从10MB降至每rank每步2.7KB）；3）设计渐进式诊断框架，通过迭代级、阶段级和内核级分析自动隔离异常窗口、落后rank和降级内核。

Result: 在10,000+ GPU生产集群上部署超过6个月，ARGUS实现了低于2%的综合开销，成功支持了持续的性能故障检测和优化。案例研究验证了其在计算落后者、链路降级、流水线气泡放大、FlashAttention JIT停顿以及被通信症状掩盖的计算落后者等多种代表性异常场景中的有效性。

Conclusion: ARGUS证明了在大规模生产环境中实现低开销、细粒度、全时在线可观测性的可行性，为LLM训练性能诊断提供了实用的解决方案，突破了传统profiler在大规模部署中的开销和存储瓶颈。

Abstract: Large-scale LLM training requires always-on, fine-grained observability for effective performance diagnosis at scale. Coarse resource monitors alone cannot localize root causes, and fine-grained profilers incur prohibitive (5%-30%) overheads and massive trace volumes, making always-on deployment impractical in large production clusters.
  We propose ARGUS, a low-overhead, fine-grained, always-on tracing and real-time analysis system for training workloads in 10,000+ GPU-scale production clusters. ARGUS decomposes observation along the training call hierarchy into CPU call stacks, framework semantics, and GPU kernel execution, with always-on collection under a combined overhead of less than 2%. It builds a unified data pipeline and compresses raw kernel events by approximately 3,700x from 10 MB to 2.7 KB per rank per step. Its progressive diagnosis framework automatically isolates anomalous windows, straggler ranks, and degraded kernels through iteration-time, phase-level, and kernel-level analysis. Deployed for over six months on a 10,000+ GPU production cluster, ARGUS has supported continuous fail-slow detection and performance optimization. Our case studies further demonstrate its effectiveness across representative anomalies, including compute stragglers, link degradation, pipeline-bubble amplification, FlashAttention JIT stalls, and compute stragglers masked by communication symptoms.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [209] [Mesh Inference: A Formal Model of Collective Intelligence Without a Center](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19537)
*Hongwei Xu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 首次提出网格推断的形式化模型，实现无中心协调下多智能体协同推断，在无隐私泄露情况下收敛至全局最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察到询问模型是能量最小化的推断过程，研究者希望建立一个无中心协调者的分布式推断框架，使多个独立智能体能够通过交换观测信息共同得出任何单一智能体无法独立获得的结论，同时保护各智能体的内部隐私状态。

Method: 将网格建模为耦合自由能系统，每个智能体局部松弛该能量；采用单一的准入/发射策略控制信息交换，所有智能体仅共享类型化的观测值而非权重、梯度或隐藏状态；在理论分析中证明耦合始终为M矩阵。

Result: 证明三个核心性质：1）网格推断对任意准入策略收敛到唯一答案；2）当贡献视图载波连通时，能够精确得出中心化最优解（识别完备性）；3）无节点传输内部状态，保密性为识别的对偶（仅观测性）。在线性高斯情形下，延迟为O(diam²)，并形式化了无中心学习循环的架构。

Conclusion: 本研究提出了首个网格推断形式化模型，证明了在适当条件下可实现与中心化方法等价的推断结果，同时保证隐私安全。开放问题是非线性情形下询问是提升集体推断还是导致自信的错误，即非线性闭包能否得出升级答案。

Abstract: We present a formal model of mesh inference: how a population of independent agents, each holding private state and exchanging only admitted, typed observations, derives a conclusion none of them holds alone, with no central coordinator and no agent exposed. No agent shares weights, gradients, or hidden state, and the agents may span different teams, networks, and organizations. Motivated by the observation that asking a model is energy-minimizing inference, we model the mesh as a coupled free energy that each agent relaxes locally. We show that a single admission/emission policy governs three properties. First, mesh inference converges to a unique answer for any admission, symmetric or not, because the coupling is always an M-matrix. Second, it is identification-complete: it derives the centralized optimum exactly when the contributing views are carrier-connected. Third, it is observation-only: no node transmits its internals, and confidentiality is the dual of identification. Content-addressed lineage is the only global side-channel. In the linear-Gaussian regime every derived answer is determined, hence equal to the centralized optimum, at O(diam^2) latency, the measured price of removing the center. One such derivation is one turn of a center-free learning loop, which we formalize as architecture rather than prove. The open problem we state is when asking improves the collective rather than corrupting it: whether the non-linear closure derives an upgraded answer or a confident error. To our knowledge, this is the first formal model of mesh inference.

</details>


### [210] [SIGMA: Skill-Incidence Graphs for Compositional Multi-Agent Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19758)
*Kun Zeng,Yu Huo,Siyu Zhang,Yuecheng Zhuo,Yuquan Lu,Haoyue Liu,Siyue Chen,Xiaoying Tang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: SIGMA框架通过将智能体构建为可复用技能的组合，而非封闭实体，解决了传统图MAS方法难以泛化到新能力组合的问题，在多个基准上超越最强基线2+分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图的多智能体系统设计方法主要通过优化预定义智能体、角色或群体之间的通信拓扑来改进协作。然而，由于每个节点仍是封闭集合实体，这些方法难以泛化到需要未见能力组合的任务。

Method: 提出SIGMA（Skill-Incidence Graph-based Multi-Agent）框架：1）给定任务和技能库，预测技能-智能体关联矩阵；2）从选定的技能组合智能体节点嵌入；3）解码构建的智能体之上的通信拓扑；4）执行时通过技能专用邮箱将消息路由到相关分配的能力，使关联结构直接可操作。

Result: 在三个基础LLM的六个推理和编程基准测试中，SIGMA达到最佳平均性能，相比最强非组合拓扑基线CARD分别提升2.06、2.36和1.75分。对未见技能库展现出更强的鲁棒性，平均性能下降仅0.96分。

Conclusion: 组合式节点构建是通信拓扑优化之外多智能体设计的一个互补且重要的轴心方向。将智能体构建为可复用技能的组合能够提升泛化能力和性能。

Abstract: Existing graph-based multi-agent system (MAS) designers mainly improve collaboration by optimizing communication topologies over predefined agents, roles, or groups. However, because each node remains a closed-set entity, these methods struggle to generalize to tasks that require unseen combinations of capabilities. We propose SIGMA, a skill-incidence graph framework that constructs agents as task-conditioned bundles of reusable skills. Given a task and a skill library, SIGMA predicts a skill-agent incidence matrix, composes agent node embeddings from selected skills, and decodes a communication topology over the constructed agents. During execution, skill-specific mailboxes route messages to the relevant assigned capabilities, making the incidence structure directly operational. Across six reasoning and coding benchmarks with three base LLMs, SIGMA achieves the best average performance and improves over CARD, the strongest non-compositional topology-based baseline, by 2.06, 2.36, and 1.75 points, respectively. It also shows stronger robustness to unseen skill libraries, with an average performance drop of only 0.96 points. These results suggest that compositional node construction is a complementary and important axis for multi-agent design beyond communication topology optimization. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SIGMA-2338/.

</details>


### [211] [Blame is easier than praise: Measuring off-ball defensive performance in football](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19931)
*Jonas Bischofberger,Runqing Ma,Pascal Bauer,Kilian Arnsmeyer,Arnold Baca*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于防守压力区域（DPA）的多智能体时空轨迹归因框架，用于量化足球球员的防守贡献，特别是防守跑位质量。该方法在跨性别、跨联赛的大规模数据集上验证有效，其指标比最佳动作类指标提升约1个标准差，是首个可靠测量防守跑位错误的足球指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有足球防守表现评估局限于抢断和拦截等离散动作，忽视了球员通过跑位产生的持续防守影响。由于缺乏球员级别的真实标签，如何量化球员防守跑位的贡献是一个未被充分研究的问题。

Method: 提出一个归因框架，通过计算防守压力区域（DPA）得出球员参与度分数，将事件级别的期望威胁变化分配给个体球员。该方法在自动检测的球队结构中计算角色条件基线，从而确定每个防守球员对通过传球造成的威胁的预期责任。在无真实标签的情况下，设计了结合多个弱代理指标的评估协议。

Result: 在大规模跨性别、跨联赛数据集（男子世界杯64场、女子德甲116场、男子德丙336场）上验证，有效性得分比最佳动作类指标提升约1个标准差。研究发现"对高价值动作的责任"指标与外部评分和球员市场价值有强相关性，证明了其测量防守跑位错误的可靠性。许多流行的防守指标显示有效性有限。

Conclusion: 该方法首次实现了足球中防守跑位错误的可靠量化，弥补了传统动作类指标的不足。所有代码已公开以支持可重复性和后续研究，为足球防守表现评估提供了新的方法论工具。

Abstract: The defensive performance of football players is commonly measured through a limited number of actions like tackles and interceptions while their continuous impact through positional behaviour has hardly been studied before. We formulate this problem as an attribution over multi-agent spatiotemporal trajectories without player-level ground truth labels, where event-level changes of expected threat are distributed among individuals. We propose a framework that performs this attribution using player involvement scores calculated from defensive pressure areas (DPAs). By computing role-conditioned baselines within automatically detected team structures, we can determine each defender's expected responsibility for threat created through arbitrary passes. The validity and robustness of this approach are evaluated on a uniquely extensive cross-gender and cross-competition data set, including positional and event data from 64 matches of the men's World Cup, 116 matches of the women's German Bundesliga and 336 matches of the men's German 3. Liga. In the absence of a ground truth, we propose an evaluation protocol that combines multiple relatively weak proxies into robust summary scores. We find a validity score that is improved by around 1 standard deviation compared to the best action-based metric and demonstrate that many popular measures show limited validity. The "blame" for conceding high-value actions shows especially strong correlations with external ratings and market values, making it the first published metric in football to reliably measure positioning errors. All code underlying this work is publicly available to support reproducibility and further research.

</details>
